\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)}\left\langle\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}.

With
g_{A,m}\equiv\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)}g
, where
\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)
is as in Notation
26
, we obtain for
each
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},m}\left(f,g\right)\right|=\left|\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right],A,m}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right|d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\left(\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right),

which is at most

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\left\|\left(\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)},

where the square function inequality in Theorem
18
shows that
the second norm is bounded by
C\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}
, and because of this we assume without loss of
generality,

(8.31)

\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}=1.

By the telescoping property of martingale differences, together with the
bound
\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)
in (
8.26
) on the averages
of
\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}^{\sigma}f
in
the tower
\left(\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right],A\right]
, we have

(8.32)

\left|\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\right|=\left|\sum_{I\in\left(\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right],A\right]:\ J\subset_{\tau}I}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left(x\right)\mathbf{1}_{A\setminus I_{J}}\left(x\right)\right|\lesssim M_{\sigma}^{\mathop{\mathrm{d}y}}\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}^{\sigma}\left(x\right)\mathbf{1}_{A\setminus S}\left(x\right)\equiv\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\left(x\right)\ .

Next we use the Monotonicity Lemma and the fact that

\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}\cap\mathcal{D}\left[S\right]\subset\left(\bigcup_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}K\right)\cup\mathcal{N}_{\tau}\left(S\right)

where
\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)
is the collection of
maximal
\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}
intervals
K
in
I
with
K\subset_{\tau}S
, and
\mathcal{N}_{\tau}\left(S\right)\equiv\left\{J\subset S:\ell\left(J\right)\geq 2^{-\tau}\ell\left(S\right)\right\}
is the set of ‘
\tau
-nearby’ dyadic intervals in
S
. Then remembering that
\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}=1
, we have the
following estimate for the sum in
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right],A,m}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}H_{\sigma}\varphi_{J}^{\mathcal{A},F,S}\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\cap K}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\mathcal{N}_{\tau}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\right)+\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{n}ear}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)},

where

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\cap K}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2},

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{n}ear}}\left(f\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{N}_{\tau}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}.

Now

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{F\setminus S}\frac{1}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,c_{J}\right)\right]^{2}}d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\int_{F\setminus S}\frac{1}{\left[\ell\left(K\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,c_{K}\right)\right]^{2}}d\sigma\left(y\right)=\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)},

for
K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)\cup\mathcal{N}_{\tau}\left(S\right)
, as one easily verifies using that

\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,c_{J}\right)\approx\ell\left(K\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,c_{K}\right)

in both cases. Thus with absolute projections, as defined in (
3.12
),

(8.33)

\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g}^{\omega},m}\right|h\equiv\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\\
J\subset K\end{subarray}}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}h\right|^{2}}\ \ \ \text{and \ \ }\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}\right|h\equiv\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(S\right):\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\\
J\subset K\end{subarray}}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}h\right|^{2}},

for
\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{Q}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)
, we can ‘lift’ the intervals
J
to
their corresponding
\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}
Whitney interval
K
, to obtain that,

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)\lesssim\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\cap K}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega},m}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2},

and hence

\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega},m}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

Using the corollary to the disjoint support Lemma
23
,
together with the geometric decay bound (
8.28
), we will now
use inequality (
8.9
) in the Corona Martingale Comparison Principle
in Proposition
34
to prove that for every
m\geq 1
,

(8.34)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega},m}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)2^{-m\delta}mN^{2}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}^{\mathcal{A},\left[N\right]}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

where
N
is as in (
8.39
).

Indeed, for
k\geq 1
,
F\in\mathcal{F}
and
Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]
, we begin by letting

\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}\equiv\left\{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(k\right)}\left(Q\right)\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)\right\},

denote the collection of triples
\left(A,S,K\right)
where
A\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(k\right)}\left(Q\right)
lies
k
levels below the top
Q
in the tree
\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]
. Note that the intervals
K
above are pairwise disjoint in
Q
for each
fixed
k
. Indeed, they are pairwise disjoint in the intervals
S
, which
are in turn pairwise disjoint in the intervals
A
, which are in turn
pairwise disjoint in the interval
Q
for each fixed
m
.

With
F\in\mathcal{F}
and
Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]
fixed, let

\displaystyle g\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{k}g_{k}\left(x\right)\text{ and }b\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{k}b_{k}\left(x\right)\ ,\ \ \ \ \text{where}

\displaystyle g_{k}\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}g_{K,k}\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega},m}Z\left(x\right),

\displaystyle b_{k}\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}b_{K,k}\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}Z\left(x\right),

\displaystyle G_{k}\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\left\{g_{K,k}\left(x\right)\right\}_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\text{ and }B_{k}\left(x\right)\equiv\left\{b_{K,k}\left(x\right)\right\}_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}.

Then for fixed
F
and
Q
, we have from (
8.24
) that the ratio in (
8.10
) is

\displaystyle M_{\mathcal{L}}^{\left(m,N\right)}\left(g,b\right)\approx\sup_{k}\sup_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\frac{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)}^{\omega}b\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{k}\sup_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\frac{\left\|\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g}^{\omega},m}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}{\left\|\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}=\sup_{k}\sup_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\frac{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g}^{\omega},m}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{k}\sup_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\frac{\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\bigcup_{B\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)}B\cap K\right]\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right)}{\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\left\{\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[K\right]\setminus\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(N+1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap K\right]\right\}\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right)}.

Now the bound,

(8.35)

\frac{\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\bigcup_{B\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)}B\cap K\right]\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right)}{\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\left\{\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[K\right]\setminus\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(N+1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap K\right]\right\}\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right)}\lesssim m2^{-m\delta},

follows directly from (
8.28
) and then applying (
8.27
). Now we write

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{m=1}^{N}+\sum_{m=N+1}^{\infty}\right\}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Sigma_{1}^{N}+\Sigma_{N+1}^{\infty},

and apply inequality (
8.9
) in the Martingale Comparison Principle
in Proposition
34
, to the functions
g
and
b
above to obtain

\displaystyle\Sigma_{N+1}^{\infty}\lesssim\sum_{m=N+1}^{\infty}\sqrt{mN2^{-m\delta}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

where

(8.36)

g_{Q}\equiv\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)}g\text{
and }g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}\equiv\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(Q\right)}g,

and where we have used (
8.35
), which gives the decay
mN2^{-m\delta}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}
when passing
from
\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)
to
\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,N}\right|Z\left(x\right)
to
\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)
.

For the finite sum, we cannot
directly
use the Martingale Comparison
Principle in Proposition
34
since
m\leq N
. On the other hand, if
we set

\Omega_{\gamma}\equiv\left\{x\in K:\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}>\gamma 2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\right\},

then we obtain from the negation of the pointwise inequality above,

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{N}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{K\setminus\Omega_{\gamma}}\left(x\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\gamma}N\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

Now note that
\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}
is constant on
K

if
m+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)>N
, and so in this case we have

\displaystyle\left|\Omega_{\gamma}\right|_{\omega}\gamma 2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{\gamma}}\left(\frac{\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}{\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{K}\left(\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)}{\int_{K}\left(\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)}\left|K\right|_{\omega}\lesssim mN2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\left|K\right|_{\omega},

which implies

\left|\Omega_{\gamma}\right|_{\omega}\leq\frac{1}{\gamma}N^{2}\left|K\right|_{\omega}<\frac{1}{2}\left|K\right|_{\omega}\text{ for }\gamma>2N^{2},

and hence that
\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{\left|K\setminus\Omega_{\gamma}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}\leq 1
. Thus in
the case that
m+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)>N
, we can apply a slight
variant of the Martingale Comparison Principle in Proposition
34
with

\displaystyle g_{k}\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}g_{K,k}\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g}^{\omega},m}Z\left(x\right),

\displaystyle b_{k}\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}b_{K,k}\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{\left(A,S,K\right)\in\Lambda_{k}^{F,Q}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right),

where
g_{k}
is unchanged, but
b_{k}
now has projection onto the larger
set of frequencies
\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}
. The
only difference in the proof of this variant of Proposition
34
, is
the use of the inequality
\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{\left|K\setminus\Omega_{\gamma}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}\leq 1
in place of the inequality (
8.19
). The factor in (
8.10
) also changes to

M_{\mathcal{L}}^{\left(m,N\right)}\left(g,b\right)\equiv\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\frac{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\bigcup_{A\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left(Q\right)}\mathcal{D}\left(K\right)}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}{\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(Q\right)}^{\omega}b\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}},

which has the bound
mN2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}
. Now we
writing the sum in shorthand form,

\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]\times\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}=\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}\ ,

we obtain,

\displaystyle\sum_{m+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)>N}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\Omega_{\gamma}}\left(x\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{2}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{Q}^{\left[N\right]}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

Finally in the case that
m+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)<N
, we have

2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\geq 2^{-\left(N-m\right)\delta}\geq 2^{-N\delta}>0,

and so we can simply use the pointwise inequality
\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)\leq\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,Q}\right|Z\left(x\right)

to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{m+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\leq N}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,m}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\Omega_{\gamma}}\left(x\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{N\delta}N\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq s\leq N\\
1\leq m\leq N\end{subarray}}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,\left(s\right)}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

These two estimates combine to bound
\Sigma_{1}^{N}
by
\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)N^{2}
times

\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq s\leq N\\
1\leq m\leq N\end{subarray}}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{5}}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A,\left(s\right)}}^{\omega}}\right|Z\left(x\right)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

Collecting all of our estimates, and using
\Lambda_{g_{A,\left(s\right)}}^{\omega}\subset\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}
, we have shown that

\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q\circ A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}2^{-m\delta}mN^{2}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{A}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{A}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}&\text{ if }&m>N\\
\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)N^{2}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{A}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{A}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}&\text{ if }&m\leq N\end{array}\right.,

where
\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{A}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{A}}}\left(x\right)
is
defined in (
8.25
) above, and
g_{Q}
is defined in (
8.36
).
Now summing in
m
yields

\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}^{m}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q\circ A},\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)\frac{N^{2}}{\delta^{2}}\max_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{A}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{A}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}.

The estimate

\left\|\sqrt{\left|\mathsf{B}\right|_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}traddle}}^{\mathcal{Q\circ A},\mathop{\mathrm{n}ear}}\left(f\right)}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)\frac{N^{2}}{\delta^{2}}\max_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{A}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{A}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

is similar but easier, since there at most
2^{\tau}
intervals that are
\tau
-nearby any given interval. This completes the proof of the Quadratic
L^{p}
-Stopping Child Lemma
37
in view of our assumption (
8.31
) that
\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}=1
.

8.5.
Completion of the proof

First, we obtain geometric decay in grandchildren from the second line in (
8.5
) and Lemma
23
,

(8.37)

\displaystyle\sum_{A^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left[A\right]}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A^{\prime}\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\sum_{A^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left[A\right]}\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A^{\prime}\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Gamma^{pm}}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\ ,\ \ \ \ \ m\geq 1.

This last estimate can be improved to

(8.38)

\sum_{A^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A^{\prime}\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\leq\frac{2}{\Gamma^{pm}}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\ ,\ \ \ \ \ m\geq 1,

provided
N
is chosen, depending only on
\Gamma
, so that

(8.39)

\frac{1}{\Gamma^{pN}}<\frac{1}{2},\ \ \ \ \ \text{e.g. }N=\left\lceil\frac{\ln 2}{\ln\Gamma^{p}}\right\rceil,

where we note for future reference that

N=\left\lceil\frac{\ln 2}{\ln\left(1+\theta\right)}\right\rceil\approx\frac{1}{\theta},\ \ \ \ \ \text{for }\Gamma^{p}=1+\theta\text{ and }0<\theta\ll 1.

Indeed, with this choice of
N
, we have by (
3.23
) and (
8.37
) with
m=N
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}+\left\|\left|\sum_{m=N+1}^{\infty}\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}+\left(\sum_{m=N+1}^{\infty}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}+\left(\sum_{A^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left(m\right)}\left(A\right)}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A^{\prime}\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}+\frac{1}{2^{\frac{1}{p}}}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\ .

Thus we obtain

(8.40)

1-\frac{1}{2^{\frac{1}{p}}}\leq\frac{\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}{\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}}\leq 1.

In particular,

\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\leq\frac{2^{\frac{1}{p}}}{2^{\frac{1}{p}}-1}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)},

implies (
8.38
) and, together with (
) implies,

(8.41)

\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\left[\mathcal{D}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}\left[A\right]\setminus\bigcup_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)}\mathcal{D}\left[S\right]\right]\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\leq\theta^{\natural}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{D}\left[A\right]\cap\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\leq\frac{2}{\left(2^{\frac{1}{p}}-1\right)^{p}}\left\|\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega}}^{\omega}\right|Z\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\ ,

for
A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]
and
Q\in\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]
, where
\mathcal{D}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}\left(A\right)\equiv\mathcal{D}\left(A\right)\setminus\left\{A\right\}
.

The stopping form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}\left(f,g\right)=\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F}}\left(f,g\right)
depends on the construction
of the stopping times
\mathcal{F}
, and is given by

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F}}\left(f,g\right)=\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}=\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F},F}\left(\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}^{\sigma}f,\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}^{\omega}g\right),

\displaystyle\text{where }\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F},F}\left(h,k\right)\equiv\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}h\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}k\right\rangle_{\omega}\ .

We will keep
f
,
g
and
\mathcal{F}

fixed
throughout our
treatment of the stopping form, although we will often consider projections
\mathsf{P}f
and
\mathsf{P}g
of
f
and
g
, and then abuse notation by
writing simply
f
or
g
instead of
\mathsf{P}f
or
\mathsf{P}g
. More
generally, for any collection of stopping times
\mathcal{A}\supset\mathcal{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F}}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(B\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\left\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A,B\in\mathcal{A}\left[F\right]\\
B\subsetneqq A\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A,B\in\mathcal{A}\left[F\right]\\
A\subsetneqq B\end{subarray}}\right\}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(B\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle+\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}A\end{subarray}}\left(E_{A_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{A}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus A_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle+\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A},\ast}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}\mathop{\mathrm{o}nly}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right),

where

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A,B\in\mathcal{A}\left[F\right]\\
B\subsetneqq A\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(B\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ ,

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{A},\ast}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A,B\in\mathcal{A}\left[F\right]\\
A\subsetneqq B\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(B\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ ,

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}\mathop{\mathrm{o}nly}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}A\end{subarray}}\left(E_{A_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{A}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus A_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ ,

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}^{\mathcal{A}}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left(I,J\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\\
J\subset_{\tau}I\end{subarray}}\left(E_{I_{J}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I}^{\sigma}f\right)\left\langle H_{\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ ,

and
\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}\left(A\right)\equiv\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(A\right)\setminus\left\{A\right\}
.

We now consider a stopping time
\mathcal{Q}\in\mathfrak{Q}
, which can by
definition be written as
\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\circ\mathcal{A}_{n+1}
for some
n\geq 0
. Then from above we have

\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(f,g\right)=\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)},\ast}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}\mathop{\mathrm{o}nly}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right),

where we can control the separated stopping form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)
using the
quadratic
L^{p}
-Stopping Child Lemma
37
. Indeed, we can use
the geometric gain in the index
m
that measures the distance between the
coronas in the separated form, i.e.

\displaystyle\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\bigcup_{B\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(m-1\right)}\left(S\right)}B\cap K\right]\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Gamma^{-pm}\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left(Q\right)\cap\left\{\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(S\right)\cap K\right]\setminus\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}\left[\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(N+1\right)}\left(S\right)\cap K\right]\right\}\cap\Lambda_{g}^{\omega};\omega\right),

\displaystyle\text{for }\left(F,Q,A,S,K\right)\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{Q}\left[F\right]\times\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}^{\mathop{\mathrm{t}rip}}\left(S\right)\text{ and }m\geq 1,

together with the equivalence (
8.40
), to obtain,

(8.42)

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)\frac{N^{2}}{\delta^{2}}\ \left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}^{\left[N\right]}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)\frac{N^{3}}{\delta^{2}}\ \sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}.

Note that the hypothesis (
8.28
) of the
L^{p}
-Stopping
Child Lemma
37
holds with
2^{-\delta}=\frac{1}{\Gamma}
by
iterating the negation of the first line in (
8.5
) of Lemma
33
, and as mentioned above, the hypothesis (
8.27
) holds by (
8.40
). Note also that

\delta=\frac{\ln\Gamma^{p}}{\ln 2}=\frac{\ln\left(1+\theta\right)}{\ln 2}\approx\theta\approx\frac{1}{N}.

The dual separated stopping form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{s}ep}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)},\ast}\left(f,g\right)
is handled
similarly, and the top only stopping form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}\mathop{\mathrm{o}nly}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)
is
handled as an easy corollary of the proof of the
L^{p}
-Stopping Child
Lemma, since there are only pairs
\left(A,J\right)
arising in this form,
and they are effectively separated by
A
itself. The only difference in the
course of the proof is that
\alpha_{\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]}\left(S\right)=\sup_{I\in\left(\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right],A\right]\cap\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}}\left|E_{I}^{\sigma}f\right|
is
replaced by
\sup_{I\in\left\{A_{J},A\right\}}\left|E_{I}^{\sigma}f\right|
.

Thus we have using
N\approx\frac{1}{\delta}\approx\frac{1}{\theta}
,
that
\left(1+2^{N\delta}\right)\frac{N^{3}}{\delta^{2}}\approx\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}
and so,

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\ \sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}+\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\ \sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}+\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n+1\right)}}\left(\mathsf{P}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}f,\mathsf{P}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}g\right)\right|,

and iteration yields

(8.43)

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F}}\left(f,g\right)\right|=\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|\leq C\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}+\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\left\{\sum_{k=0}^{n}\sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\right\}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}+\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(f,g\right)\right|,

for
n\in\mathbb{N}
, where we have suppressed the projections
\mathsf{P}^{\mathop{\mathrm{n}o}\mathop{\mathrm{t}op}}
that accummulate as we iterate. Once the lemma in
the next subsubsection is proved, we are done since
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(f,g\right)
vanishes for
n

sufficiently large because of the finite Haar support assumptions on
f
and

g
, and then using
\min\left\{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}},\frac{4-p}{2p}\right\}>0
for
1<p<4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top}}^{\mathcal{F}}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sup_{0\leq s\leq N}\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{A}}^{\omega}\right\}_{A\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta^{5}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\min\left\{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}},\frac{4-p}{2p}\right\}}\right)\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{p,\theta}\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}\left(\omega\right)},

where
C_{p,\theta}<\infty
provided
C_{p}\theta<1
. Thus we see that
the stopping form is controlled by the scalar testing characteristic
\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)
, which is of
course at most
\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\ell^{2},\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)
.

8.5.1.
The decay lemma

Here is the final lemma of the paper.

Lemma 38
.

Let
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
0\leq s\leq N
, and
1<p<4
. There is
C_{p}>0
such that for all
0<\theta<1
, there is a positive
constant
B_{\theta}
such that,

(8.44)

\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{Q\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}B_{\theta}\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{\frac{n}{p^{\prime}}}\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}&\text{ if }&1<p\leq 2\\
B_{\theta}\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\frac{4-p}{2p}}\mathfrak{T}_{H,p}^{\mathop{\mathrm{l}oc}}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}&\text{ if }&2\leq p<4\end{array}\right..

Proof.

Fix
0\leq s\leq N
throughout the following arguments. We begin with the
function

h\left(x\right)\equiv\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\sum_{A\in\mathcal{A}^{\left(s\right)}\left[Q\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)}\sum_{K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}2^{-\frac{1}{2}\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right),

where

g_{Q}\equiv\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\left(Q\right)}^{\omega}g\text{ for }Q\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},

and the associated sequence associated with
h
and
\mathcal{A}
,

\left\{2^{-\frac{1}{2}\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(A,Q\right)\delta}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right)\right\}_{Q,A,S,K\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}\times\mathcal{A}\left[Q\right]\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}.

Note that
\left\|h\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}=\left\|\left|f\right|_{\mathcal{F},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)},\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(Q\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}\right\}_{Q\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}
. In analogy with (
8.30
), we note from the
definition in (
8.25
) that the projection

\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q}}^{\omega}:\ J\subset_{\tau}\Lambda_{f}^{\sigma}\left[S\right]\\
J\subset K\end{subarray}}\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}

vanishes unless
K\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{A}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(A\right)\cap\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)
.

Now recall the definition of an iterated martingale difference sequence
\left\{h_{k}\right\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
from subsubsection
3.5.2
, where for an iterated stopping time
\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A}
,
we defined in (
3.14
) and (
3.15
), the martingale
differences
h_{k}
, the maximal depths
D_{k}
, and the iterated corona
distance
\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}{}_{\mathcal{Q}\circ\mathcal{A}}\left(A,T\right)=\mathop{\mathrm{x}dist}\ _{\mathcal{A}}\left(A,T\right)
from the root
T
to
A\in\mathcal{A}
. In Conclusion
22
we referred to this
construction there as the
regularization
of the ‘standard’ definition
of the
\mathcal{A}
-corona martingale difference sequence given in
Definition
20
.

Now we apply this regularization to the multiply iterated stopping times
\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}=\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}\circ\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}\circ...\circ\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
. We then
have the following
regularizing
property. If
Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
has associated tower

(8.45)

\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\left(Q_{n-1}\right)\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-2\right)}}\left(Q_{n-2}\right)\subset...\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}\left(Q_{1}\right)\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}}\left(Q_{0}\right),

then

\mathop{\mathrm{x}dist}\ _{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n},T\right)=D_{1}+D_{2}+...+D_{n-1}+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}{}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n},Q_{n-1}\right),

where the
D_{k}
are defined using the single iteration
\mathcal{I}^{\left(n-1\right)}\circ\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
where
\mathcal{I}^{\left(n-1\right)}\equiv\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}\circ\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}\circ...\circ\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}
. This
regularizing property makes it easier to track levels in the stopping
collection
\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
in terms of levels within each
corona
\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}\left(Q_{k}\right)
,
0\leq k\leq n
, when it comes time for estimates later on. So, keeping in
mind that we write
\mathcal{A}=\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
and
A=Q_{n}

interchangeably, we will now use the
iterated
martingale difference
sequence associated with the stopping times
\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
, which we write as,

\left\{h_{k}\left(x\right)\right\}_{k\in\mathbb{N}}=\left\{\sum_{\left(S,K\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(Q_{n}\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q_{n-1}}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right)\right\}_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\\
\mathop{\mathrm{i}tdist}{}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n},T\right)=k\end{subarray}}

where
Q_{n-1}
is determined in terms of
Q_{n}
by (
8.45
). This
iterated martingale difference sequence has the martingale property because
the projections
\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(Q_{n}\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{Q_{n-1}}}^{\omega}}
have pairwise disjoint Haar supports. In
addition, recall the pair
\left(d_{1}\left(t\right),d_{2}\left(t\right)\right)
that in the present case of an
n
-fold iteration, becomes an
n
-tuple
\left(d_{1}\left(t\right),d_{2}\left(t\right),...,d_{n}\left(t\right)\right)
associated to
A\in\mathcal{A}
with
t=\mathop{\mathrm{x}dist}\ _{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(A,T\right)
and
d_{k}\left(t\right)=\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)
.

Recall that for each
Q\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}
in the sum
above, and each
A\in\mathcal{A}\left[Q_{n-1}\right]
, we relabel
A
as
Q_{n}
and
\mathcal{A}
as
\mathcal{A}_{n}
so that there is a tower of
coronas (
8.45
), which we repeat here,

(8.46)

\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\left(Q_{n-1}\right)\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-2\right)}}\left(Q_{n-2}\right)\subset...\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(1\right)}}\left(Q_{1}\right)\subset\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}}\left(Q_{0}\right),

with
A=Q_{n}\subset Q=Q_{n-1}\subset Q_{n-2}\subset...\subset Q_{1}\subset Q_{0}
. Now define

\Omega\left[Q_{n-1}\right]\equiv\left\{\left(S,K\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\left(Q_{n-1}\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)\right\}.

Then for
K\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)
with
Q_{n}\in\mathcal{A}_{n}\left[Q_{n-1}\right]
and
S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}_{n}}\left(Q_{n}\right)
, i.e.
\left(S,K\right)\in\Omega\left[Q_{n-1}\right]
, the projection
\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}Z\left(x\right)

vanishes unless
K\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q\right)=\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)
,
in which case there is
C_{p}>0
such that for
2\leq j\leq n
,

\left\|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-1\right)}}\left(Q_{j-1}\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{{}_{Q_{n-2}}}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\leq C_{p}\theta^{\natural}2^{-\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{j-1},Q_{j-2}\right)\delta}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n-2\right)}}\left(Q_{n-2}\right)\cap\Lambda_{g_{{}_{Q_{j-3}}}}^{\omega}}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p},

since
K\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(j-1\right)}}\left(Q_{j-1}\right)
.

Define

(8.47)

\Psi_{Q_{n}}\equiv 2^{-\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)\delta},\ \ \ \ \ \text{for }Q=Q_{n}\in\mathcal{Q}_{n},

where
\left\{Q_{k}\right\}_{k=1}^{n}
is the tower of intervals
Q_{k}\in\mathfrak{C}_{S^{\left(k-1\right)}}\left(Q_{k-1}\right)
for
1\leq k\leq n
. Note that

(8.48)

\Psi_{A}=2^{-d\left(t\right)\delta},\ \ \ \ \ \text{where }d\left(t\right)\equiv\sum_{k=1}^{n}d_{k}\left(t\right)\text{ and }t=\mathop{\mathrm{x}dist}\ _{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(A,T\right).

For convenience we set

B_{n}\left(S,K\right)\equiv\Psi_{Q_{n}}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right).

It follows that for each
Q_{n}\in\mathcal{A}
and
S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}
,

(8.49)

\left\|\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}\lesssim\left(C_{p}\theta^{\natural}\right)^{n}\left\|B_{n}\left(S,K\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(0\right)}}\left(Q_{0}\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p},

where
\left\{Q_{k}\right\}_{k=1}^{n}
is the tower associated with
Q_{n}
. As a consequence, for each pair
Q_{n}\in\mathcal{A}
and
S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(Q_{n}\right)
, the factor
2^{-\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)\right)\delta}
is at most one
and becomes smaller as the distances
\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)
grow. Since summing over all
Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}
can be reindexed as summing over all towers in (
8.46
), we will consequently write
\sum_{Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}=\sum_{\left\{Q_{k}\right\}_{k=0}^{n}}
interchangeably
depending on context. We now estimate the norm
\left\|h\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}
separately in the cases
1<p\leq 2
and
2\leq p<4
, beginning with
2\leq p<4
.

The case
2\leq p<4
. In this case
\theta^{\natural}=\theta

and this will be reflected in what follows. We now claim that for
2\leq p<4
,

(8.50)

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\left\{\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right\}_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\right|_{\ell^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}^{p}\ ,

or more succinctly,

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{K\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)\lesssim\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}^{p},

where

c_{F,A,S,K}\equiv\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right),

and where the tower
\left\{Q_{k}\right\}_{k=0}^{n}
is determined from
Q_{k}=A
and
Q_{0}=F\in\mathcal{F}
.

Using the iterated corona decomposition, we have for
\eta>0
to be chosen
later,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right),

where
d\left(t\right)
is defined in (
8.48
), and we write
\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}
to mean that
A
is
t
levels
below
T
in the iterated stopping time construction, i.e.
t=\mathop{\mathrm{x}dist}{}_{\mathcal{A}}\left(A,T\right)
. Typically,
d\left(t\right)
is much
smaller that
t
, and this is what gives rise to large negative powers of
\theta
below. By Hölder’s inequality with exponent
\frac{p}{2}
, this
is at most

(8.51)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left[\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right]^{\frac{p}{2}}\right)^{\frac{2}{p}}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left[\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right]^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{2}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right).

Now we compute

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{2}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{2}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{2}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n}2^{-d\left(t\right)\delta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{d\left(t\right)\left(\eta\frac{p}{2}-\delta\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right),

which gives,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}2^{d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{2}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}-1}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{d\left(t\right)\left(\eta\frac{p}{2}-\delta\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right),

where

\left[\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\eta\frac{p}{p-2}}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}-1}\approx\left(\frac{C_{p}}{\eta}\right)^{n\left(\frac{p}{2}-1\right)},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{d\left(t\right)\left(\eta\frac{p}{2}-\delta\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}:K\sim t}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}:K\sim t}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right),

provided
\eta<\frac{2\delta}{p}
(note that we are only using
2^{d\left(t\right)\left(\eta\frac{p}{2}-\delta\right)}\leq 1
here).

Indeed, we can bound the sum of the decay factors
2^{-d\left(t\right)\delta}=\Psi_{Q^{\prime}}=2^{-\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{k}^{\prime},Q_{k-1}^{\prime}\right)\delta}
by setting
j_{k}=\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(Q_{k}^{\prime},Q_{k-1}^{\prime}\right)
and
computing,

(8.52)

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-d\left(t\right)\beta}=\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}2^{-\sum_{k=1}^{n}d_{k}\left(t\right)\beta}\leq\prod_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{j_{k}=0}^{\infty}2^{-j_{k}\beta}=\prod_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{1-2^{-\beta}}\sim\left(\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{n}.

So with for example
\eta=\frac{\delta}{p}<\frac{2\delta}{p}
, we get
altogether that,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{2}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{A}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\ \mathbf{1}_{K}\left(x\right)\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n}\left(\frac{C_{p}}{\delta}\right)^{n\left(\frac{p}{2}-1\right)}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega_{t}^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n}\left(\frac{C_{p}}{\theta}\right)^{n\left(\frac{p}{2}-1\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)\ .

Thus we are left to bound the term,

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}c_{F,A,S,K}^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\sum_{\left(F,A,S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\alpha_{A}\left(S\right)^{p}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{K}^{\Lambda_{g_{F}}}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]}\sum_{\left(S,K\right)\in\Omega\left[Q_{n}\right]}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)^{p}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right).

Using

(8.53)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{J\ \mathop{\mathrm{a}ppears}\ \mathop{\mathrm{i}n}\ \mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|K\right|_{\omega}\left(\frac{1}{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}\int_{K}\left(\sum_{J^{\prime}\subset K}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime}}^{\omega}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right)\right)=\left|K\right|_{\omega}\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(K,\omega\right)^{p}

together with (
6.4
),

(8.54)

\sup_{K\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)\cap\mathcal{D}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood}}^{\mathop{\mathrm{c}hild}}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus K}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{p}\mathsf{E}_{p}\left(K,\omega\right)^{p}\frac{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}{\left|K\right|_{\sigma}}\leq\mathfrak{X}_{F;p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p},

and the boundedness of
M_{\sigma}^{\mathop{\mathrm{d}y}}
, and the square function
estimate in Theorem
18
, we can finally bound the above
integral by

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]}\sum_{\left(S,K\right)\in\Omega\left[Q_{n}\right]}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)^{p}\left|K\right|_{\sigma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{F\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{Q_{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]}\sum_{S\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left[Q_{n}\right]}\left|M_{\sigma}\left(\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f\right)\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n}\right)\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n}\right)\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\sigma\left(x\right)\ \ \ \ \ \text{(since }p>2\text{)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta\right)^{n\left(2-\frac{p}{2}\right)}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)}^{p}.

The case
1<p\leq 2
. In this case
\theta^{\natural}=\theta^{\frac{p}{2}}
. We have, recalling that
\Omega^{4}\equiv\mathcal{F}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}\left[F\right]\times\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)\times\mathcal{W}_{\mathop{\mathrm{g}ood},\tau}\left(S\right)
,

\displaystyle\left\|h\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\omega\right)}^{p}=\left\|\left\{\Psi_{Q_{n}}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right\}_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\right\|_{L^{p}\left(\ell^{2};\omega\right)}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\left|\Psi_{Q_{n}}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\omega\left(x\right),

which using
1<p\leq 2
, is at most

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\left|\Psi_{Q_{n}}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta^{\frac{p}{2}}\right)^{n}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\Psi_{Q_{n}}^{p}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)^{p}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}\left(K,\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus S}\sigma\right)}{\ell\left(K\right)}\right)^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{S;K}^{\omega,\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}\left(F\right)}Z\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\omega\left(x\right),

where the sequence
\left\{Q_{k}\right\}_{k=1}^{n-1}
is the tower
associated with
Q_{n-1}
. Now using (
8.53
) and (
8.54
), we
bound the above integral by

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta^{\frac{p}{2}}\right)^{n}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\Psi_{Q_{n}}^{p}\alpha_{Q_{n}}\left(S\right)^{p}\left|K\right|_{\sigma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta^{\frac{p}{2}}\right)^{n}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\Psi_{Q_{n}}^{p}\left|M_{\sigma}\left(\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f\right)\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(C_{p}\theta^{\frac{p}{2}}\right)^{n}\mathfrak{X}_{\mathcal{F};p}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)^{p}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(F,Q_{n},S,K\right)\in\Omega^{4}}\Psi_{Q_{n}}^{p}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right),

where the last line follows from the boundedness of
M_{\sigma}^{\mathop{\mathrm{d}y}}

on
L^{p}\left(\sigma\right)
.

Again, we will exploit the decay of the factors
\Psi_{Q_{n}}
, as well as
the orthogonality hiding in the corona projections
\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(n\right)}}\left(Q_{n}\right)}^{\sigma}f
. We
successively apply the following inequalities for
1\leq k\leq n
,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(Q_{k-1},Q_{k}\right)\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}\left[Q_{k-1}\right]}2^{-p\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)\right)\delta}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}\left(Q_{k}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{\left(Q_{k-1},Q_{k}\right)\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}\left[Q_{k-1}\right]}\left(2^{-p\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(\left(Q_{k},Q_{k-1}\right)\right)\delta}\right)^{\frac{2}{2-p}}\right)^{\frac{2-p}{2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\left(\sum_{\left(Q_{k-1},Q_{k}\right)\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)}\times\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}\left[Q_{k-1}\right]}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k\right)}}\left(Q_{k}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}d\sigma\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{\frac{2-p}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{\left(Q_{k-1}\right)\in\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)}}\left|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{S}^{\left(k-1\right)}}\left(Q_{k-1}\right)}^{\sigma}f\left(x\right)\right|^{p}d\sigma\left(x\right),

to obtain
